local function __LPE_NATIVE_BROKER(adapters) local type_ref,pcall_ref,pairs_ref=type,pcall,pairs if type_ref(adapters)~='table'then error('NATIVE-BROKER-ADAPTERS',0)end local visible,scalar,vector,remove=adapters.visible,adapters.scalar,adapters.vector,adapters.remove if type_ref(visible)~='function'or type_ref(scalar)~='function'or type_ref(vector)~='function'or type_ref(remove)~='function'then error('NATIVE-BROKER-ADAPTERS',0)end local MAX=64;local pending,last=nil,-1 local function intact()  return type==type_ref and pcall==pcall_ref and pairs==pairs_ref and adapters.visible==visible and adapters.scalar==scalar and adapters.vector==vector and adapters.remove==remove end if not intact()then error('NATIVE-BROKER-TAMPER',0)end local function value_ok(v)local t=type_ref(v);return t=='boolean'or t=='number'or t=='string'end local function id_ok(v)local t=type_ref(v);return t=='number'or t=='string'end local function metric(k,n)local g=_G;local m=g and g.__VM_METRICS__;if type_ref(m)=='table'then m[k]=(m[k]or 0)+(n or 1)end end local function push(snapshot)  if not intact()then return false,'tamper'end  if type_ref(snapshot)~='table'then return false,'snapshot'end  local seq,count,data=snapshot.seq,snapshot.count,snapshot.data  if type_ref(seq)~='number'or seq~=seq or seq%1~=0 or seq<0 then return false,'sequence'end  if seq<=last or(pending and seq<=pending.seq)then return false,'stale'end  if type_ref(count)~='number'or count%1~=0 or count<0 or count>MAX*5 or type_ref(data)~='table'then return false,'snapshot'end  local copy={}  for k,v in pairs_ref(data)do   if type_ref(k)~='number'or k%1~=0 or k<1 or k>count or not value_ok(v)then return false,'snapshot-data'end   copy[k]=v  end  for i=1,count do if copy[i]==nil then return false,'snapshot-data'end end  pending={seq=seq,count=count,data=copy};return true,seq end local function validate(batch,seq)  if type_ref(batch)~='table'or batch.seq~=seq then return false,'batch'end  local count,data=batch.count,batch.data  if type_ref(count)~='number'or count%1~=0 or count<0 or count>MAX or type_ref(data)~='table'then return false,'batch'end  local limit=count*5  for k in pairs_ref(data)do if type_ref(k)~='number'or k%1~=0 or k<1 or k>limit then return false,'batch-data'end end  for i=0,count-1 do   local p=i*5+1;local op,id,a,b,c=data[p],data[p+1],data[p+2],data[p+3],data[p+4]   if type_ref(op)~='number'or op%1~=0 or not id_ok(id)then return false,'command'end   if op==1 then if type_ref(a)~='boolean'or b~=nil or c~=nil then return false,'visible'end   elseif op==2 then if not value_ok(a)or b~=nil or c~=nil then return false,'scalar'end   elseif op==3 then if type_ref(a)~='number'or type_ref(b)~='number'or type_ref(c)~='number'then return false,'vector'end   elseif op==4 then if a~=nil or b~=nil or c~=nil then return false,'remove'end   else return false,'opcode'end  end  return true,count,data end local function flush(policy)  if not intact()then return false,'tamper'end  if pending==nil then return true,0,last end  if type_ref(policy)~='function'then return false,'policy'end  local snapshot=pending;pending=nil;metric('bridge_batch')  local ok,batch=pcall_ref(policy,snapshot)  if not ok then if pending==nil then pending=snapshot end;return false,batch end  local valid,count,data=validate(batch,snapshot.seq)  if not valid then if pending==nil then pending=snapshot end;return false,count end  for i=0,count-1 do   local p=i*5+1;local op,id,a,b,c=data[p],data[p+1],data[p+2],data[p+3],data[p+4];local applied,why   if op==1 then applied,why=pcall_ref(visible,id,a)   elseif op==2 then applied,why=pcall_ref(scalar,id,a)   elseif op==3 then applied,why=pcall_ref(vector,id,a,b,c)   else applied,why=pcall_ref(remove,id)end   if not applied then return false,why end  end  last=snapshot.seq;metric('command_batch');metric('command',count);return true,count,last end local function submit(snapshot,policy)local ok,why=push(snapshot);if not ok then return false,why end;return flush(policy)end return{push=push,flush=flush,submit=submit,pending=function()return pending and pending.seq or nil end,last=function()return last end,max=MAX} end local v=M[s:byte(i)]if v==nil then error('POOL-BASE64-CHAR',0)end;acc=acc*64+v;bits=bits+6;if bits>=8 then bits=bits-8;out[#out+1]=string.char(math.floor(acc/2^bits)%256);acc=acc%2^bits end end;return KDa_q(x,1,5,9,13);KDa_q(x,2,6,10,14);KDa_q(x,3,7,11,15);KDa_q(x,4,8,12,16);KDa_q(x,1,6,11,16);KDa_q(x,2,7,12,13);KDa_q(x,3,8,9,14);KDa_q(x,4,5,10,15)end;local nbytes=math.min(64,#s-base+1);for
